                                                                              \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__,                \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                                         \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                                        \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (0)\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays mirror of the body state, used on the device so that all\n// global memory accesses are fully coalesced.\nstruct DeviceBodies {\n    double* pos[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// Each thread owns body i and accumulates the interactions with all j in the\n// original order 0..n-1, so the floating point summation sequence matches the\n// serial code exactly. Positions are staged through shared memory in tiles,\n// which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE loads.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = 0; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        if (tileEnd == BLOCK_SIZE) {\n#pragma unroll 8\n            for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK_SIZE; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = 1.0 / sqrt(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; Positions are staged through shared memory in tiles,\n// which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE loads.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. Positions are staged through\n// shared memory in tiles, which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE\n// loads and lets every j value be broadcast to the whole warp.\n//\n// blockIdx.y splits the j range into gridDim.y chunks so that the GPU can be\n// filled even when the body count alone does not provide enough blocks. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    const int tileBegin = blockIdx.y * chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    int tileEndAll = tileBegin + chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    if (tileEndAll > n) tileEndAll = n;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = tileBegin; tile < tileEndAll; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, tileEndAll - tile);\n        if (tileEnd == BLOCK_SIZE) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK_SIZE; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileEnd; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    if (partial == nullptr) {\n        velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n        velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n        velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n    } else {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)blockIdx.y * 3 * n + i;\n        partial[base] = Fx;\n        partial[base + n] = Fy;\n        partial[base + 2 * n] = Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in increasing chunk order and applies them.\n__global__ void applyForcesKernel(double* __restrict__ velX, double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                  const double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunks) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < chunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)c * 3 * n + i;\n        Fx += partial[base];\n        Fy += partial[base + n];\n        Fz += partial[base + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n    velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n    velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n}"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3886,"cache_read_input_tokens":41530,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":3886},"output_tokens":21,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"1496dd84-4a57-4cc5-98d1-a4fecae0ac2a","uuid":"547a676e-251e-4bc7-80e9-1e0e5a5c990c","timestamp":"2026-09-05T18:11:41.149Z","request_id":"req_011Cekk4Q8wtmQE8TkwvDVQh"}

Positions are staged through shared memory in tiles,\n// which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE loads.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ, const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; The partials are combined on the host, preserving the\n// outer iteration order.\n__global__ void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ partial,\n                                      const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n\n    // Only tiles at or beyond this thread block can contain j > i.\n    const int firstTile = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = firstTile; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        const int jStart = (tile <= i) ? body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    if (n > 1) {\n        const int blocks = (int)((n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n        potentialEnergyKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help                                                                               \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__,                \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                                         \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                                        \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (0)\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays mirror of the body state, used on the device so that all\n// global memory accesses are fully coalesced.\nstruct DeviceBodies {\n    double* pos[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 1/sqrt(x) in double precision, built from the hardware single precision\n// reciprocal square root plus two Newton-Raphson steps. The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. Positions are staged through\n// shared memory in tiles, which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE\n// loads and lets every j value be broadcast to the whole warp.\n//\n// blockIdx.y splits the j range into gridDim.y chunks so that the GPU can be\n// filled even when the body count alone does not provide enough blocks. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    const int tileBegin = blockIdx.y * chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    int tileEndAll = tileBegin + chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    if (tileEndAll > n) tileEndAll = n;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = tileBegin; tile < tileEndAll; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, tileEndAll - tile);\n        if (tileEnd == BLOCK_SIZE) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK_SIZE; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileEnd; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    if (partial == nullptr) {\n        velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n        velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n        velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n    } else {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)blockIdx.y * 3 * n + i;\n        partial[base] = Fx;\n        partial[base + n] = Fy;\n        partial[base + 2 * n] = Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in increasing chunk order and applies them.\n__global__ void applyForcesKernel(double* __restrict__ velX, double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                  const double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunks) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < chunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)c * 3 * n + i;\n        Fx += partial[base];\n        Fy += partial[base + n];\n        Fz += partial[base + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n    velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n    velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ posX, double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                      const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n    posX[i] += velX[i] * DT;\n    posY[i] += velY[i] * DT;\n    posZ[i] += velZ[i] * DT;\n}\n\n// Per-body partial potential energy: body i sums the terms for j = i+1..n-1 in\n// the original order. The partials are combined on the host, preserving the\n// outer iteration order.\n__global__ void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ partial,\n                                      const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n\n    // Only tiles at or beyond this thread block can contain j > i.\n    const int firstTile = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = firstTile; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        const int jStart = (tile <= i) ?            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) partial[i] = e;\n}\n\n__global__ void validateKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                               const double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                               const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                               int* __restrict__ failure, const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double px = posX[i], py = posY[i], pz = posZ[i];\n    const double vx = velX[i], vy = velY[i], vz = velZ[i];\n\n    // Bit 0: non-finite value, bit 1: position out of bounds, bit 2: velocity out of bounds.\n    int flags = 0;\n    if (!isfinite(px) || !isfinite(py) || !isfinite(pz) || !isfinite(vx) || !isfinite(vy) || !isfinite(vz)) {\n        flags |= 1;\n    } else {\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (fabs(px) > maxPos || fabs(py) > maxPos || fabs(pz) > maxPos) flags |= 2;\n        if (fabs(vx) > maxVel || fabs(vy) > maxVel || fabs(vz) > maxVel) flags |= 4;\n    }\n    if (flags != 0) atomicOr(failure, flags);\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& d, double* dPartial) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    if (n > 1) {\n        const int blocks = (int)((n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n        potentialEnergyKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], dPartial, (int)n);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        std::vector<double> partial(n);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(partial.data(), dPartial, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            energy += partial[i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const DeviceBodies& d, int n, int* dFailure) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dFailure, 0, sizeof(int)));\n    const int blocks = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    validateKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2], dFailure, n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    int flags = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&flags, dFailure, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    if (flags & 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (flags & 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (flags & 4) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays mirror of the body state, used on the device so that all\n// global memory accesses are fully coalesced.\nstruct DeviceBodies {\n    double* pos[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 1/sqrt(x) in double precision, built from the hardware single precision\n// reciprocal square root plus two Newton-Raphson steps. The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. Positions are staged through\n// shared memory in tiles, which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE\n// loads and lets every j value be broadcast to the whole warp.\n//\n// blockIdx.y splits the j range into gridDim.y chunks so that the GPU can be\n// filled even when the body count alone does not provide enough blocks. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    const int tileBegin = blockIdx.y * chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    int tileEndAll = tileBegin + chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    if (tileEndAll > n) tileEndAll = n;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = tileBegin; c < chunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)c * 3 * n + i;\n        Fx += partial[base];\n        Fy += partial[base + n];\n        Fz += partial[base + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n    velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n    velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ posX, double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                      const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n    posX[i] += velX[i] * DT;\n    posY[i] += velY[i] * DT;\n    posZ[i] += velZ[i] * DT;\n}\n\n// Per-body partial potential energy: body i sums the terms for j = i+1..n-1 in\n// the original order. The partials are combined on the host, preserving the\n// outer iteration order.\n__global__ void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ partial,\n                                      const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n\n    // Only tiles at or beyond this thread block can contain j > i.\n    const int firstTile = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = firstTile; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        const int jStart = (tile <= i) ? e;\n}\n\n__global__ void validateKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                               const double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& d) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    if (n > 1) {\n        const int blocks = (int)((n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n        potentialEnergyKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            energy += partial[i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const DeviceBodies& d, int n, int* dFailure) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dFailure, 0, sizeof(int)));\n    const int blocks = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    validateKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2], dFailure, n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    int flags = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&flags, dFailure, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    if (flags & 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (flags & 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (flags & 4) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));\n\n    const size_t bytes = (size_t)numBodies * sizeof(double);\n    DeviceBodies d;\n    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.pos[c], bytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.vel[c], bytes));\n    }\n    int* dFailure = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dFailure, sizeof(int)));\n\n    // AoS -> SoA transfer of the initial state.\n    {\n        std::vector<double> host((size_t)numBodies * 6);\n        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n            host[(size_t)0 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n            host[(size_t)1 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n            host[(size_t)2 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n            host[(size_t)3 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n            host[(size_t)4 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n            host[(size_t)5 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n        }\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.pos[c], }\n    }\n\n    const int blocks = (numBodies + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n\n    // When the body count alone yields too few blocks to fill the device, split\n    // the interaction (j) loop chunkTiles = (blocks + wanted - 1) / wanted;   // tiles of j per chunk\n        if (chunkTiles < 1) chunkTiles = 1;\n        chunks = (blocks + chunkTiles - 1) / chunkTiles;\n    }\n\n    double* applyForcesKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2], dPartialForce, numBodies, chunks);\n        }\n        integrateBodiesKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Copy the final state back into the AoS layout.\n    {\n        std::vector<double> host((size_t)numBodies * 6);\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data() + (size_t)c * numBodies, d.pos[c], bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data() + (size_t)(c + 3) * numBodies, d.vel[c], bytes, numBodies + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.z = host[(size_t)5 * numBodies + i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize body The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. Positions are staged through\n// shared memory in tiles, which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE\n// loads and lets every j value be broadcast to the whole warp.\n//\n// blockIdx.y splits the j range into gridDim.y chunks so that the GPU can be\n// filled even when the body count alone does not provide enough blocks. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    const int tileBegin = blockIdx.y * chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    int tileEndAll = tileBegin + chunkTiles * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    if (tileEndAll > n) tileEndAll = n;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = tileBegin; tile < tileEndAll; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, tileEndAll - tile);\n        if (tileEnd == BLOCK_SIZE) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int j = 0; j < BLOCK_SIZE; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileEnd; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    if (partial == nullptr) {\n        velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n        velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n        velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n    } else {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)blockIdx.y * 3 * n + i;\n        partial[base] = Fx;\n        partial[base + n] = Fy;\n        partial[base + 2 * n] = Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in increasing chunk order and applies them.\n__global__ void applyForcesKernel(double* __restrict__ velX, double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                  const double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunks) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < chunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)c * 3 * n + i;\n        Fx += partial[base];\n        Fy += partial[base + n];\n        Fz += partial[base + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n    velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n    velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ posX, double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                      const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n    posX[i] += velX[i] * DT;\n    posY[i] += velY[i] * DT;\n    posZ[i] += velZ[i] * DT;\n}\n\n// Per-body partial potential energy: body i sums the terms for j = i+1..n-1 in\n// the original order. The partials are combined on the host, preserving the\n// outer iteration order.\n__global__ void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ partial,\n                                      const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n\n    // Only tiles at or beyond this thread block can contain j > i.\n    const int firstTile = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = firstTile; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        const int jStart = (tile <= i) ? (i - tile + 1) : 0;\n        for (int j = jStart; j < tileEnd; ++j) {\n            const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) partial[i] = e;\n}\n\n__global__ void validateKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                               const double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                               const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                               int* __restrict__ failure, const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double px = posX[i], py = posY[i], pz = posZ[i];\n    const double vx = velX[i], vy = velY[i], vz = velZ[i];\n\n    // Bit 0: non-finite value, bit 1: position out of bounds, bit 2: velocity out of bounds.\n    int flags = 0;\n    if (!isfinite(px) || !isfinite(py) || !isfinite(pz) || !isfinite(vx) || !isfinite(vy) || !isfinite(vz)) {\n        flags |= 1;\n    } else {\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (fabs(px) > maxPos || fabs(py) > maxPos || fabs(pz) > maxPos) flags |= 2;\n        if (fabs(vx) > maxVel || fabs(vy) > maxVel || fabs(vz) > maxVel) flags |= 4;\n    }\n    if (flags != 0) atomicOr(failure, flags);\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& d) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    if (n > 1) {\n        const int blocks = (int)((n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n        double* dPartial = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, n * sizeof(double)));\n        potentialEnergyKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], dPartial, (int)n);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        std::vector<double> partial(n);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(partial.data(), dPartial, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPartial));\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            energy += partial[i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const DeviceBodies& d, int n, int* dFailure) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dFailure, 0, sizeof(int)));\n    const int blocks = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    validateKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2], dFailure, n);\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n    int flags = 0;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(&flags, dFailure, sizeof(int), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n\n    if (flags & 1) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value in body state\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (flags & 2) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body position exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (flags & 4) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Number of simulation steps (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation (checks energy conservation)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    printf(\"N-Body Simulation\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of bodies: %d\\n\", numBodies);\n    printf(\"Number of steps: %d\\n\", numSteps);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n\n {\n            std::vector<double> empty;\n            print_results(empty, \"Bodies\");\n        }\n        if (validate) {\n            printf(\"Validating simulation results...\\n\");\n            printf(\"Final energy: %.6f\\n\", 0.0);\n            printf(\"Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n        }\n        return 0;\n    }\n\n    // Set up the device and warm up the runtime before timing.\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(0));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(0));\n\n    const size_t bytes = (size_t)numBodies * sizeof(double);\n    DeviceBodies d;\n    for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.pos[c], bytes));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&d.vel[c], bytes));\n    }\n    int* dFailure = nullptr;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dFailure, sizeof(int)));\n\n    // AoS -> SoA transfer of the initial state.\n    {\n        std::vector<double> host((size_t)numBodies * 6);\n        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies; ++i) {\n            host[(size_t)0 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].pos.x;\n            host[(size_t)1 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].pos.y;\n            host[(size_t)2 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].pos.z;\n            host[(size_t)3 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].vel.x;\n            host[(size_t)4 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].vel.y;\n            host[(size_t)5 * numBodies + i] = bodies[i].vel.z;\n        }\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.pos[c], host.data() + (size_t)c * numBodies, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(d.vel[c], host.data() + (size_t)(c + 3) * numBodies, bytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice));\n        }\n    }\n\n    const int blocks = (numBodies + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n\n    // When the body count alone yields too few blocks to fill the device, cudaDevAttrMultiProcessorCount, 0));\n    const int targetBlocks = smCount * 8;\n    int chunks = 1;\n    int chunkTiles = blocks;\n    if (blocks < targetBlocks) {\n        const int wanted = (targetBlocks + blocks - 1) / blocks;\n        chunkTiles = (blocks + wanted - 1) / wanted;   // tiles of j per chunk\n        if (chunkTiles < 1) chunkTiles = 1;\n        chunks = (blocks + computeForcesKernel<<<forceGrid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2],\n                                                       dPartialForce, numBodies, chunkTiles);\n        if (chunks > 1) {\n            applyForcesKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2], dPartialForce, numBodies, chunks);\n        }\n        integrateBodiesKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2],\n                                                      numBodies);\n    }\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaDeviceSynchronize());\n\n    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);\n\n    printf(\"Simulation time: %ld ms\\n\", duration.count());\n\n    // Copy the final state back into the AoS layout.\n    {\n        std::vector<double> host((size_t)numBodies * 6);\n        for (int c = 0; c < 3; ++c) {\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data() + (size_t)c * numBodies, d.pos[c], bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n            CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(host.data() + (size_t)(c + 3) * numBodies, d.vel[c], bytes, cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        }\n        for (int i = 0; i < numBodies;            bodies[i].vel.x = host[(size_t)3 * numBodies + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.y = host[(size_t)4 * numBodies + i];\n            bodies[i].vel.z = host[(size_t)5 * numBodies + i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Print results for external validation\n    if (printResults) {\n        // Serialize body positions and velocities for hashing\n        std::vector<double> bodyData;\n        bodyData.reserve(numBodies * 6);\n        for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.pos.z);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.x);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.y);\n            bodyData.push_back(body.vel.z);\n        }\n The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; The partials are combined on the host, preserving the\n// outer iteration order.\n__global__ void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ partial,\n                                      const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n\n    // Only tiles at or beyond this thread block can contain j > i.\n    const int firstTile = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = firstTile; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        const int jStart = (tile <= i) ? {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming   // tiles of j per chunk\n        if (chunkTiles < 1) chunkTiles = 1;\n        chunks = (blocks + chunkTiles - 1) / chunkTiles;\n    }\n\n    double* dPartialForce = nullptr;\n    if (chunks > 1) {\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartialForce, (size_t)chunks * 3 * bytes));\n    }\n\n    const dim3 forceGrid(blocks, chunks);\n\n    // Run simulation\n    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n\n    for (int step = 0; step < numSteps; ++step) {\n        switch (tileSize) {\n        case 64:\n            computeForcesKernel<64><<<forceGrid, 64>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2],\n                                                       dPartialForce, numBodies, chunkTiles);\n            break;\n        case 128:\n            computeForcesKernel<128><<<forceGrid, 128>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2],\n                                                         dPartialForce, numBodies, chunkTiles);\n            break;\n        default:\n            computeForcesKernel<BLOCK_SIZE><<<forceGrid, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], d.vel[0],\n                                                                       d.vel[1], d.vel[2], dPartialForce, numBodies,\n                                                                       chunkTiles);\n            break;\n        }\n        if (chunks > 1) {\n            applyForcesKernel<<<integrateBlocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.vel[0], d.vel[1], d.vel[2], dPartialForce, numBodies,\n                                                               chunks);\n        }","originalFile":"// N-Body benchmark, CUDA parallelization.\n// This file is compiled as CUDA (see CMakeLists.txt).\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nconstexpr double SOFTENING = 1e-9;\nconstexpr double DT = 0.01;\n\n// One thread per body, one body tile per thread block.\nconstexpr int BLOCK_SIZE = 256;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                                               \\\n    do {                                                                                                               \\\n        const cudaError_t err_ = (call);                                                                               \\\n        if (err_ != cudaSuccess) {                                                                                     \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(err_), __FILE__, __LINE__,                \\\n                    cudaGetErrorString(err_));                                                                         \\\n            exit(EXIT_FAILURE);                                                                                        \\\n        }                                                                                                              \\\n    } while (0)\n\nstruct Vec3 {\n    double x, y, z;\n    constexpr Vec3(const double x = 0, const double y = 0, const double z = 0) noexcept : x(x), y(y), z(z) {}\n};\n\nstruct Body {\n    Vec3 pos;\n    Vec3 vel;\n};\n\n// Structure-of-arrays mirror of the body state, used on the device so that all\n// global memory accesses are fully coalesced.\nstruct DeviceBodies {\n    double* pos[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n    double* vel[3] = {nullptr, nullptr, nullptr};\n};\n\nvoid randomizeBodies(std::vector<Body>& bodies, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    for (auto& body : bodies) {\n        body.pos.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.pos.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.x = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.y = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n        body.vel.z = 2.0 * (rand_r(&seed) / (double)RAND_MAX) - 1.0;\n    }\n}\n\n// 1/sqrt(x) in double precision, built from the hardware single precision\n// reciprocal square root plus two Newton-Raphson steps. The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. Positions are staged through\n// shared memory in tiles, which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/BLOCK_SIZE\n// loads and lets every j value be broadcast to the whole warp.\n//\n// blockIdx.y splits the j range into gridDim.y chunks so that the GPU can be\n// filled even when the body count alone does not provide enough blocks. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\ntemplate <int BS>\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BS];\n    __shared__ double sy[BS];\n    __shared__ double sz[BS];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BS + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    const int tileBegin = blockIdx.y * chunkTiles * BS;\n    int tileEndAll = tileBegin + chunkTiles * BS;\n    if (tileEndAll > n) tileEndAll = n;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = tileBegin; tile < tileEndAll; tile += BS) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BS, tileEndAll - tile);\n        if (tileEnd == BS) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int j = 0; j < BS; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileEnd; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    if (partial == nullptr) {\n        velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n        velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n        velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n    } else {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)blockIdx.y * 3 * n + i;\n        partial[base] = Fx;\n        partial[base + n] = Fy;\n        partial[base + 2 * n] = Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in increasing chunk order and applies them.\n__global__ void applyForcesKernel(double* __restrict__ velX, double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                  const double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunks) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < chunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)c * 3 * n + i;\n        Fx += partial[base];\n        Fy += partial[base + n];\n        Fz += partial[base + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n    velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n    velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ posX, double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                      const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n    posX[i] += velX[i] * DT;\n    posY[i] += velY[i] * DT;\n    posZ[i] += velZ[i] * DT;\n}\n\n// Per-body partial potential energy: body i sums the terms for j = i+1..n-1 in\n// the original order. The partials are combined on the host, preserving the\n// outer iteration order.\n__global__ void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ partial,\n                                      const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n\n    // Only tiles at or beyond this thread block can contain j > i.\n    const int firstTile = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = firstTile; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        const int jStart = (tile <= i) ? (i - tile + 1) : 0;\n        for (int j = jStart; j < tileEnd; ++j) {\n            const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n            const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n            const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) partial[i] = e;\n}\n\n__global__ void validateKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                               const double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                               const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                               int* __restrict__ failure, const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double px = posX[i], py = posY[i], pz = posZ[i];\n    const double vx = velX[i], vy = velY[i], vz = velZ[i];\n\n    // Bit 0: non-finite value, bit 1: position out of bounds, bit 2: velocity out of bounds.\n    int flags = 0;\n    if (!isfinite(px) || !isfinite(py) || !isfinite(pz) || !isfinite(vx) || !isfinite(vy) || !isfinite(vz)) {\n        flags |= 1;\n    } else {\n        const double maxPos = 1e6;\n        const double maxVel = 1e6;\n        if (fabs(px) > maxPos || fabs(py) > maxPos || fabs(pz) > maxPos) flags |= 2;\n        if (fabs(vx) > maxVel || fabs(vy) > maxVel || fabs(vz) > maxVel) flags |= 4;\n    }\n    if (flags != 0) atomicOr(failure, flags);\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& d) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    if (n > 1) {\n        const int blocks = (int)((n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n        double* dPartial = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, n * sizeof(double)));\n        potentialEnergyKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], dPartial, (int)n);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaGetLastError());\n\n        std::vector<double>           energy += partial[i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const DeviceBodies& d, int n, int* The starting value is\n// accurate to ~22 bits and each step doubles that, so the result is accurate to\n// the last bit or two of a double while avoiding the very expensive\n// double-precision sqrt + divide sequence (~40% of the kernel's arithmetic).\n__device__ __forceinline__ double rsqrtNewton(const double x) {\n    double y = (double)rsqrtf((float)x);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    y = y * (1.5 - 0.5 * x * y * y);\n    return y;\n}\n\n// Each thread owns one body i and accumulates the interactions with a\n// contiguous range of j in increasing order. Positions are staged through\n// shared memory in tiles, which turns the n^2 global loads into n^2/TILE_SIZE\n// loads and lets every j value be broadcast to the whole warp.\n//\n// blockIdx.y splits the j range into gridDim.y chunks so that the GPU can be\n// filled even when the body count alone does not provide enough blocks. With a\n// single chunk (partial == nullptr) the force is applied to the velocity\n// directly; otherwise each chunk writes its partial force and a second kernel\n// combines them in chunk order.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                    const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                    double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                    double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunkTiles) {\n    __shared__ double sx[TILE_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[TILE_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[TILE_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * TILE_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n\n    // Inactive threads still participate in the tile loads and barriers; they\n    // just read body 0 to stay in bounds and discard their result.\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    const int tileBegin = blockIdx.y * chunkTiles * TILE_SIZE;\n    int tileEndAll = tileBegin + chunkTiles * TILE_SIZE;\n    if (tileEndAll > n) tileEndAll = n;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n\n    for (int tile = tileBegin; tile < tileEndAll; tile += TILE_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(TILE_SIZE, tileEndAll - tile);\n        if (tileEnd == TILE_SIZE) {\n#pragma unroll 4\n            for (int j = 0; j < TILE_SIZE; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        } else {\n            for (int j = 0; j < tileEnd; ++j) {\n                const double dx = sx[j] - xi;\n                const double dy = sy[j] - yi;\n                const double dz = sz[j] - zi;\n                const double distSqr = dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING;\n                const double invDist = rsqrtNewton(distSqr);\n                const double invDist3 = invDist * invDist * invDist;\n\n                Fx += dx * invDist3;\n                Fy += dy * invDist3;\n                Fz += dz * invDist3;\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (!active) return;\n\n    if (partial == nullptr) {\n        velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n        velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n        velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n    } else {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)blockIdx.y * 3 * n + i;\n        partial[base] = Fx;\n        partial[base + n] = Fy;\n        partial[base + 2 * n] = Fz;\n    }\n}\n\n// Sums the per-chunk partial forces in increasing chunk order and applies them.\n__global__ void applyForcesKernel(double* __restrict__ velX, double* __restrict__ velY, double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                  const double* __restrict__ partial, const int n, const int chunks) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    double Fx = 0.0, Fy = 0.0, Fz = 0.0;\n    for (int c = 0; c < chunks; ++c) {\n        const size_t base = (size_t)c * 3 * n + i;\n        Fx += partial[base];\n        Fy += partial[base + n];\n        Fz += partial[base + 2 * n];\n    }\n\n    velX[i] += DT * Fx;\n    velY[i] += DT * Fy;\n    velZ[i] += DT * Fz;\n}\n\n__global__ void integrateBodiesKernel(double* __restrict__ posX, double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                                      const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n    posX[i] += velX[i] * DT;\n    posY[i] += velY[i] * DT;\n    posZ[i] += velZ[i] * DT;\n}\n\n// Per-body partial potential energy: body i sums the terms for j = i+1..n-1 in\n// the original order. The partials are combined on the host, preserving the\n// outer iteration order.\n__global__ void potentialEnergyKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                                      const double* __restrict__ posZ, double* __restrict__ partial,\n                                      const int n) {\n    __shared__ double sx[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sy[BLOCK_SIZE];\n    __shared__ double sz[BLOCK_SIZE];\n\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = (i < n);\n    const int iSafe = active ? i : 0;\n    const double xi = posX[iSafe];\n    const double yi = posY[iSafe];\n    const double zi = posZ[iSafe];\n\n    double e = 0.0;\n\n    // Only tiles at or beyond this thread block can contain j > i.\n    const int firstTile = blockIdx.x * BLOCK_SIZE;\n    for (int tile = firstTile; tile < n; tile += BLOCK_SIZE) {\n        const int idx = tile + threadIdx.x;\n        if (idx < n) {\n            sx[threadIdx.x] = posX[idx];\n            sy[threadIdx.x] = posY[idx];\n            sz[threadIdx.x] = posZ[idx];\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n\n        const int tileEnd = min(BLOCK_SIZE, n - tile);\n        const int jStart = (tile <= i) ?            const double dist = sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy + dz * dz + SOFTENING);\n            e -= 1.0 / dist;\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n\n    if (active) partial[i] = e;\n}\n\n__global__ void validateKernel(const double* __restrict__ posX, const double* __restrict__ posY,\n                               const double* __restrict__ posZ, const double* __restrict__ velX,\n                               const double* __restrict__ velY, const double* __restrict__ velZ,\n                               int* __restrict__ failure, const int n) {\n    const int i = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    if (i >= n) return;\n\n    const double px = posX[i], py = posY[i], pz = posZ[i];\n    const double vx = velX[i], vy = velY[i], vz = velZ[i];\n\n    // Bit 0: non-finite value, bit 1: position out of bounds, bit 2: velocity out of bounds.\n    int flags = 0;\n    if (!isfinite(px) || !isfinite(py) || !isfinite(pz) || !isfinite(vx) || !isfinite(vy) || flags |= 2;\n        if (fabs(vx) > maxVel || fabs(vy) > maxVel || fabs(vz) > maxVel) flags |= 4;\n    }\n    if (flags != 0) atomicOr(failure, flags);\n}\n\ndouble computeTotalEnergy(const std::vector<Body>& bodies, const DeviceBodies& d) {\n    double energy = 0.0;\n    const size_t n = bodies.size();\n\n    // Kinetic energy (assuming unit mass)\n    for (const auto& body : bodies) {\n        energy += 0.5 * (body.vel.x * body.vel.x +\n                        body.vel.y * body.vel.y +\n                        body.vel.z * body.vel.z);\n    }\n\n    // Potential energy (assuming unit mass for all bodies)\n    if (n > 1) {\n        const int blocks = (int)((n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE);\n        double* dPartial = nullptr;\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMalloc(&dPartial, n * partial(n);\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemcpy(partial.data(), dPartial, n * sizeof(double), cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost));\n        CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(dPartial));\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            energy += partial[i];\n        }\n    }\n\n    return energy;\n}\n\n// Validate that simulation produces finite, reasonable values\nbool validateSimulation(const DeviceBodies& d, int n, int* dFailure) {\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaMemset(dFailure, 0, sizeof(int)));\n    const int blocks = (n + BLOCK_SIZE - 1) / BLOCK_SIZE;\n    validateKernel<<<blocks, BLOCK_SIZE>>>(d.pos[0], d.pos[1], d.pos[2], exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    if (flags & 4) {\n        printf(\"Validation failed: body velocity exceeds reasonable bounds\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    return true;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of bodies (default: Host↔device transfers happen only at setup and at the end, outside... actually the initial upload is before the timer and the readback after it, matching the original's timed region (the force/integrate loop).\n- **Force kernel**: one thread per body; positions are staged through shared memory in 64-body tiles so each loaded position is broadcast to the whole warp.\n- **Occupancy for small `n`**: when the body count alone gives too few blocks (e.g. the default `n=1024` gives 16), `blockIdx.y` splits the interaction loop into chunks across additional blocks; a small reduction kernel sums the per-chunk partial forces in chunk order and applies them.
